HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000532/B002","root_001175/B005","root_001583/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_1a6bae34f5fbd17ce390","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"31:5:3\",\"root_001583\",[\"B002\"]],[\"31:5:5\",\"root_000532\",[\"B002\"]],[\"31:5:8\",\"root_001175\",[\"B005\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Doğru yol, üzerinde yürüyeni taşıyan ve Rabbin aşamalı bakımıyla hedefe geliştiren bir zemin gibi işler.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000532/B002","root_001175/B001","root_001175/B003","root_001175/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_3bb5cf95e0ce8d484bc3","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"31:5:5\",\"root_000532\",[\"B002\"]],[\"31:5:8\",\"root_001175\",[\"B001\",\"B003\",\"B005\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Kurtuluş, yalnızca sonuca ulaşmak değil, kalıcı iyiliği yetiştirmek için zemini yaran ve işleyen bir çabayı da içerir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000532/B002","root_001175/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_45f3c29c4198a149c56a","text":"2. **Success as cultivation through cleaving.** **ف ل ح B001** (cleaving) and **B003** (the tiller who splits ground) remain visible beneath **B005** (lasting good); **ر ب ب B002** provides nurture. The movement from nurturing source to *mufliḥūn* supports a material analogy: prepared ground must be opened before growth can persist. The causal arrow from disciplined opening to fruit is inferred. Success therefore includes the strenuous cultivation that makes durable good possible, not merely the final attainment.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_8ce02da7c9efe6e0665d","text":"- 31:10 supplied literal earth, rain, and germination. That later ecology was the strongest surprise in the window: it activated the tilling branch of `ف ل ح` and made being `عَلَىٰ هُدًى` resemble standing on cultivated, supporting ground.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001583/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_90d5faa09c2b30d666b4","text":"1. **Guidance as load-bearing ground.** **ه د ي B002** supplies course and intended direction, while **ر ب ب B002** supplies nurture and completion and **ف ل ح B005** lasting success. The spatial *ʿalā* places the people “upon” guidance, and the source phrase assigns its production to nurturing lordship. I infer that guidance bears and develops the traveler until the outcome lasts. The verse reads less like possession of instructions and more like being carried by a cultivated route.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001583/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_944ea8d26ecaa4199f51","text":"1. **Guidance as cultivated supporting ground.** Lexical evidence: `ه د ي B002` is a course or intended direction, `ر ب ب B002` is stage-by-stage nurture and completion, and `ف ل ح B001/B003` is cleaving and tilling the ground. Structural cue: `عَلَىٰ هُدًى` spatially places the group upon guidance; 31:10 later supplies earth, rain, germination, and noble plant kinds. Assigned role: guidance is the ground/course, lordly nurture supplies cultivation, and the successful are the resulting tillers or harvesters. Abductive move (inferred): the later material ecology activates the dormant agricultural mechanism in `مفلحون`. Reading change: success reads as growth produced by sustained cultivation on a divinely nurtured course.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001583/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_ade600754fd4bed6511c","text":"2. **Success as reaching the aim of a traversed route.** Lexical evidence: `ه د ي B002` also carries intended direction, and `ف ل ح B005` is attaining one's aim with lasting good. Structural cue: the demonstrative is repeated on both sides of the ayah, while 31:6 immediately opposes this group with someone who diverts people from `سَبِيلِ ٱللَّهِ`. Assigned role: the repeated identification binds being on the course to arriving at its proper end. Abductive move (inferred): a stable route is what converts direction into attainment. Reading change: `ٱلْمُفْلِحُونَ` is not a static title; it names those whose present bearing is already aligned with a durable destination.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_da96b1d39d7f9a439ceb","text":"Ayah 31:10’s rain-driven growth retrospectively strengthened the tiller branch of **ف ل ح**, while 31:31’s vessel kept on course by favor sharpened “upon guidance” as supported travel.
